require('./services/schedulingEngine');\\n\\ntest('toMinutes converts HH:MM into minutes', () => {\\n  assert.equal(toMinutes('09:30'), 570);\\n  assert.equal(toMinutes('17:00'), start_time: '09:00',\\n    end_time: '11:00',\\n    break_start: '10:00',\\n    break_end: '10:30',\\n    appointment_duration: 30,\\n    buffer_minutes: 0\\n  };\\n\\n  const appointments = [\\n    { appointments, '2026-09-17');\\n  assert.deepEqual(slots, ['09:30']);\\n});\\n\\ntest('isAppointmentConflict catches overlapping bookings', () => {\\n  const existing = [\\n    { start_time: {"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_koBlolHpQZLwBslkONLg7IHi","toolName":"create_file","arguments":{"filePath":"/workspaces/medslot/server/src/schedulingEngine.test.js","content":"const test = require('node:test');\nconst assert = require('node:assert/strict');\n\nconst {\n  toMinutes,\n  overlaps,\n  generateSlots,\n  getAvailableSlots,\n  isAppointmentConflict\n} = require('./services/schedulingEngine');\n\ntest('toMinutes converts HH:MM into minutes', () => {\n  assert.equal(toMinutes('09:30'), 570);\n  assert.equal(toMinutes('17:00'), 1020);\n});\n\ntest('overlaps detects conflicting time ranges', () => {\n  assert.equal(overlaps('09:00', '09:30', '09:15', '09:45'), true);\n  assert.equal(overlaps('09:00', '09:30', '09:30', '10:00'), false);\n});\n\ntest('generateSlots creates all valid consultation slots and omits break time', () => {\n  const slots = generateSlots('09:00', '11:00', 30, '10:00', '10:30');\n  assert.deepEqual(slots, [\n    '09:00',\n    '09:30',\n    '10:30',\n    '11:00'\n  ]);\n});\n\ntest('getAvailableSlots filters out booked appointments', () => {\n  const doctor = {\n    start_time: '09:00',\n    end_time: '11:00',\n    break_start: '10:00',\n    break_end: '10:30',\n    appointment_duration: 30,\n    buffer_minutes: 0\n  };\n\n  const appointments = [\n    { start_time: '2026-09-17T09:00:00', end_time: '2026-09-17T09:30:00' },\n    { start_time: '2026-09-17T10:30:00', end_time: '2026-09-17T11:00:00' }\n  ];\n\n  const slots = getAvailableSlots(doctor, appointments, '2026-09-17');\n  assert.deepEqual(slots, ['09:30']);\n});\n\ntest('isAppointmentConflict catches overlapping bookings', () => {\n  const existing = [\n    { start_time: '2026-09-17T10:00:00', end_time: '2026-09-17T10:30:00' }\n  ];\n\n  assert.equal(\n    isAppointmentConflict('2026-09-17T09:45:00', '2026-09-17T10:15:00', existing),\n    true\n  );\n\n  assert.equal(\n    isAppointmentConflict('2026-09-17T10:30:00', '2026-09-17T11:00:00', existing),\n    false\n  );\n});\n"}},"id":"d9cdd948-e900-4700-82eb-8b187fa20b7f","timestamp":"2026-09-17T09:40:59.184Z","parentId":"6dc8323f-fcd2-4edc-a411-b72721205d85"}

toMinutes(timeString) {\\n  if (!timeString) return 0;\\n  if (typeof timeString === 'number') return timeString;\\n\\n  const [hours, minutes] = String(timeString).split(':').map(Number);\\n  return hours * 60 + minutes;\\n}\\n\\nfunction formatTime(totalMinutes) {\\n  const safeMinutes = ((totalMinutes % (24 * 60)) + (24 * 60)) % (24 * 60);\\n  const hours = Math.floor(safeMinutes / 60);\\n  const minutes = safeMinutes % 60;\\n  return `${String(hours).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(minutes).padStart(2, '0')}`;\\n}\\n\\nfunction overlaps(startA, endA, startB, endB) {\\n  const firstStart = startA instanceof Date ? startA.getTime() : new Date(startA).getTime();\\n  const firstEnd = endA instanceof Date ? endA.getTime() : new Date(endA).getTime();\\n  const secondStart = startB instanceof Date ? startB.getTime() : new Date(startB).getTime();\\n  const secondEnd = endB instanceof Date ? endB.getTime() : new Date(endB).getTime();\\n\\n  if (Number.isNaN(firstStart) || Number.isNaN(firstEnd) || Number.isNaN(secondStart) || Number.isNaN(secondEnd)) {\\n    const aStart = toMinutes(startA);\\n    const aEnd = toMinutes(endA);\\n    const bStart = toMinutes(startB);\\n    const bEnd = toMinutes(endB);\\n    return aStart < bEnd && aEnd > bStart;\\n  }\\n\\n  return firstStart < secondEnd && firstEnd > secondStart;\\n}\\n\\nfunction generateSlots(startTime, endTime, durationMinutes, breakStart, breakEnd) {\\n  const startMinutes = toMinutes(startTime);\\n  const endMinutes = toMinutes(endTime);\\n  const breakStartMinutes = breakStart ? toMinutes(breakStart) : null;\\n  const breakEndMinutes = breakEnd ? toMinutes(breakEnd) : null;\\n\\n  const slots = [];\\n\\n  for (let slotMinutes = startMinutes; slotMinutes <= endMinutes; slotMinutes += durationMinutes) {\\n    const slotEndMinutes = slotMinutes + durationMinutes;\\n\\n    const isInsideBreak =\\n      breakStartMinutes !== null &&\\n      breakEndMinutes !== null &&\\n      slotMinutes < breakEndMinutes &&\\n      slotEndMinutes > breakStartMinutes;\\n\\n    if (slotMinutes > endMinutes) continue;\\n    if (isInsideBreak) continue;\\n\\n    slots.push(formatTime(slotMinutes));\\n  }\\n\\n  return slots;\\n}\\n\\nfunction getAvailableSlots(doctor, appointments = [], dateString) {\\n  const normalizedDate = dateString || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);\\n  const dayStart = `${normalizedDate}T00:00:00`;\\n  const dayEnd = `${normalizedDate}T23:59:59`;\\n\\n  const baseSlots = generateSlots(\\n    doctor.start_time,\\n    doctor.end_time,\\n    Number(doctor.appointment_duration || 30),\\n    doctor.break_start,\\n    doctor.break_end\\n  );\\n\\n  return baseSlots.filter((slot) => {\\n    const slotStart = new Date(`${normalizedDate}T${slot}:00`);\\n    const slotEnd = new Date(slotStart.getTime() + (Number(doctor.appointment_duration || 30) * 60000));\\n\\n    if (slotStart.getTime() < new Date(dayStart).getTime() || slotEnd.getTime() > new Date(dayEnd).getTime() + 1000) {\\n      return false;\\n    }\\n\\n    return !(appointments || []).some((appointment) => {\\n      if (!appointment || appointment.status === 'CANCELLED') return false;\\n      const appointmentStart = new Date(appointment.start_time);\\n      const appointmentEnd = new Date(appointment.end_time);\\n      return overlaps(slotStart, slotEnd, appointmentStart, appointmentEnd);\\n    });\\n  });\\n}\\n\\nfunction isAppointmentConflict(newStart, newEnd, existingAppointments = []) {\\n  const nextStart = new Date(newStart).getTime();\\n  const nextEnd = new Date(newEnd).getTime();\\n\\n  return existingAppointments.some((appointment) => {\\n    if (!appointment || appointment.status === 'CANCELLED') return false;\\n    const appointmentStart = new Date(appointment.start_time).getTime();\\n    const appointmentEnd = new Date(appointment.end_time).getTime();\\n    return nextStart < appointmentEnd && nextEnd > appointmentStart;\\n  });\\n}\\n\\nmodule.exports = {\\n  toMinutes,\\n  formatTime,\\n  overlaps,\\n  generateSlots,\\n  getAvailableSlots,\\n  isAppointmentConflict\\n};\\n\"}","type":"function"},{"toolCallId":"call_JFTVgkwr09Whk8UIrgxq83Lf","name":"create_file","arguments":"{\"filePath\":\"/workspaces/medslot/server/src/controllers/clinicController.js\",\"content\":\"const db = require('../database/db');\\nconst { getAvailableSlots, isAppointmentConflict } = require('../services/schedulingEngine');\\n\\nfunction normalizeDoctorInput(payload) {\\n  return {\\n    name: String(payload.name || '').trim(),\\n    specialization: String(payload.specialization || '').trim(),\\n    start_time: payload.start_time || '09:00',\\n    end_time: payload.end_time || '17:00',\\n    break_start: payload.break_start || null,\\n    break_end: payload.break_end || null,\\n    appointment_duration: Number(payload.appointment_duration || 30),\\n    buffer_minutes: Number(payload.buffer_minutes || 0)\\n  };\\n}\\n\\nfunction listDoctors(req, res) {\\n  try {\\n    const doctors = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM doctors ORDER BY name ASC').all();\\n    res.json({ success: true, doctors });\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    console.error('List doctors error:', error);\\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to load doctors' });\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nfunction createDoctor(req, res) {\\n  try {\\n    const doctor = normalizeDoctorInput(req.body);\\n\\n    if (!doctor.name) {\\n      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Doctor name is required' });\\n    }\\n\\n    const result = db.prepare(`\\n      INSERT INTO doctors (\\n        name, specialization, start_time, end_time, break_start, break_end,\\n        appointment_duration, buffer_minutes\\n      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\\n    `).run(\\n      doctor.name,\\n      doctor.specialization || '',\\n      doctor.start_time,\\n      doctor.end_time,\\n      doctor.break_start,\\n      doctor.break_end,\\n      doctor.appointment_duration,\\n      doctor.buffer_minutes\\n    );\\n\\n    const createdDoctor = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM doctors WHERE id = ?').get(result.lastInsertRowid);\\n\\n    res.status(201).json({ success: true, message: 'Doctor created', doctor: createdDoctor });\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    console.error('Create doctor error:', error);\\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to create doctor' });\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nfunction listPatients(req, res) {\\n  try {\\n    const patients = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM patients ORDER BY created_at DESC').all();\\n    res.json({ success: true, patients });\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    console.error('List patients error:', error);\\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to load patients' });\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nfunction createPatient(req, res) {\\n  try {\\n    const { name, phone, email } = req.body;\\n\\n    if (!name || !phone) {\\n      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Name and phone are required' });\\n    }\\n\\n    const result = db.prepare(`\\n      INSERT INTO patients (name, phone, email)\\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?)\\n    `).run(name.trim(), phone.trim(), email ? email.trim() : null);\\n\\n    const patient = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM patients WHERE id = ?').get(result.lastInsertRowid);\\n    res.status(201).json({ success: true, message: 'Patient created', patient });\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    console.error('Create patient error:', error);\\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to create patient' });\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nfunction listAppointments(req, res) {\\n  try {\\n    const { date } = req.query;\\n    let query = `\\n      SELECT a.*, d.name AS doctor_name, p.name AS patient_name\\n      FROM appointments a\\n      JOIN doctors d ON d.id = a.doctor_id\\n      JOIN patients p ON p.id = a.patient_id\\n    `;\\n    const params = [];\\n\\n    if (date) {\\n      query += ' WHERE date(a.start_time) = ?';\\n      params.push(date);\\n    }\\n\\n    query += ' ORDER BY a.start_time ASC';\\n\\n    const appointments = db.prepare(query).all(...params);\\n    res.json({ success: true, appointments });\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    console.error('List appointments error:', error);\\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to load appointments' });\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nfunction getAvailability(req, res) {\\n  try {\\n    const { doctorId, date } = req.query;\\n\\n    if (!doctorId || !date) {\\n      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'doctorId and date are required' });\\n    }\\n\\n    const doctor = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM doctors WHERE id = ?').get(Number(doctorId));\\n    if (!doctor) {\\n      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Doctor not found' });\\n    }\\n\\n    const appointments = db.prepare(`\\n      SELECT * FROM appointments\\n      WHERE doctor_id = ? AND date(start_time) = ? AND status != 'CANCELLED'\\n      ORDER BY start_time ASC\\n    `).all(Number(doctorId), date);\\n\\n    const slots = getAvailableSlots(doctor, appointments, date);\\n    res.json({ success: true, doctor, date, slots });\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    console.error('Get availability error:', error);\\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to fetch availability' });\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nfunction createAppointment(req, res) {\\n  try {\\n    const { doctor_id, patient_id, date, time } = req.body;\\n\\n    if (!doctor_id || !patient_id || !date || !time) {\\n      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Doctor, patient, date and time are required' });\\n    }\\n\\n    const doctor = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM doctors WHERE id = ?').get(Number(doctor_id));\\n    const patient = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM patients WHERE id = ?').get(Number(patient_id));\\n\\n    if (!doctor || !patient) {\\n      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Doctor or patient not found' });\\n    }\\n\\n    const startDateTime = new Date(`${date}T${time}:00`);\\n    const durationMinutes = Number(doctor.appointment_duration || 30);\\n    const endDateTime = new Date(startDateTime.getTime() + (durationMinutes * 60 * 1000));\\n\\n    const existingAppointments = db.prepare(`\\n      SELECT * FROM appointments\\n      WHERE doctor_id = ? AND status != 'CANCELLED' AND start_time < ? AND end_time > ?\\n    `).all(Number(doctor_id), endDateTime.toISOString(), startDateTime.toISOString());\\n\\n    if (isAppointmentConflict(startDateTime.toISOString(), endDateTime.toISOString(), existingAppointments)) {\\n      return res.status(409).json({ success: false, message: 'Selected time conflicts with an existing appointment' });\\n    }\\n\\n    const result = db.prepare(`\\n      INSERT INTO appointments (doctor_id, patient_id, start_time, end_time)\\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\\n    `).run(\\n      Number(doctor_id),\\n      Number(patient_id),\\n      startDateTime.toISOString(),\\n      endDateTime.toISOString()\\n    );\\n\\n    const appointment = db.prepare(`\\n      SELECT a.*, d.name AS doctor_name, p.name AS patient_name\\n      FROM appointments a\\n      JOIN doctors d ON d.id = a.doctor_id\\n      JOIN patients p ON p.id = a.patient_id\\n      WHERE a.id = ?\\n    `).get(result.lastInsertRowid);\\n\\n    res.status(201).json({ success: true, message: 'Appointment booked', appointment });\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    console.error('Create appointment error:', error);\\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to book appointment' });\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nfunction cancelAppointment(req, res) {\\n  try {\\n    const { id } = req.params;\\n    const { reason } = req.body;\\n\\n    const appointment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM appointments WHERE id = ?').get(Number(id));\\n    if (!appointment) {\\n      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Appointment not found' });\\n    }\\n\\n    const cancellationFee = 0;\\n    const updated = db.prepare(`\\n      UPDATE appointments\\n      SET status = 'CANCELLED', cancellation_time = ?, cancellation_reason = ?, cancellation_fee = ?\\n      WHERE id = ?\\n    `).run(new Date().toISOString(), reason || 'Cancelled by staff', cancellationFee, Number(id));\\n\\n    if (updated.changes === 0) {\\n      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Unable to cancel appointment' });\\n    }\\n\\n    const cancelledAppointment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM appointments WHERE id = ?').get(Number(id));\\n    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Appointment cancelled', appointment: cancelledAppointment });\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    console.error('Cancel appointment error:', error);\\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to cancel appointment' });\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nmodule.exports = {\\n  listDoctors,\\n  createDoctor,\\n  listPatients,\\n cancelAppointment\\n};\\n\"}","type":"function"},{"toolCallId":"call_dcKcrQYNJP1Xg2z3T7ax03tE","name":"create_file","arguments":"{\"filePath\":\"/workspaces/medslot/server/src/routes/clinicRoutes.js\",\"content\":\"const listAppointments,\\n  getAvailability,\\n  createAppointment,\\n  cancelAppointment\\n} = require('../controllers/clinicController');\\n\\nconst router = express.Router();\\n\\nrouter.get('/doctors', breakStart, breakEnd) {\n  const startMinutes = toMinutes(startTime);\n  const endMinutes = toMinutes(endTime);\n  const breakStartMinutes = breakStart ? toMinutes(breakStart) : null;\n  const breakEndMinutes = breakEnd ? toMinutes(breakEnd) : null;\n\n  const slots = [];\n\n  for (let slotMinutes = startMinutes; slotMinutes <= endMinutes; slotMinutes += durationMinutes) {\n    const slotEndMinutes = slotMinutes + durationMinutes;\n\n    const isInsideBreak =\n      breakStartMinutes !== null &&\n      breakEndMinutes !== null &&\n      slotMinutes < breakEndMinutes &&\n      slotEndMinutes > breakStartMinutes;\n\n    if (slotMinutes > endMinutes) continue;\n    if (isInsideBreak) continue;\n\n    slots.push(formatTime(slotMinutes));\n  }\n\n  return slots;\n}\n\nfunction getAvailableSlots(doctor, appointments = [], dateString) {\n  const normalizedDate = dateString || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);\n  const dayStart = `${normalizedDate}T00:00:00`;\n  const dayEnd = `${normalizedDate}T23:59:59`;\n\n  const baseSlots = generateSlots(\n    doctor.start_time,\n    doctor.end_time,\n    Number(doctor.appointment_duration || 30),\n    doctor.break_start,\n    doctor.break_end\n  );\n\n  return baseSlots.filter((slot) => {\n    const slotStart = new Date(`${normalizedDate}T${slot}:00`);\n    const slotEnd = new Date(slotStart.getTime() + (Number(doctor.appointment_duration || 30) * 60000));\n\n    if (slotStart.getTime() < new Date(dayStart).getTime() || slotEnd.getTime() > new Date(dayEnd).getTime() + 1000) {\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    return !(appointments || []).some((appointment) => {\n      if (!appointment || appointment.status === 'CANCELLED') return false;\n      const appointmentStart = new Date(appointment.start_time);\n      const appointmentEnd = new Date(appointment.end_time);\n      return overlaps(slotStart, slotEnd, appointmentStart, appointmentEnd);\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nfunction isAppointmentConflict(newStart, newEnd, existingAppointments = []) {\n  const nextStart = new Date(newStart).getTime();\n  const nextEnd = new Date(newEnd).getTime();\n\n  return existingAppointments.some((appointment) => {\n    if (!appointment || appointment.status === 'CANCELLED') return false;\n    const appointmentStart = new Date(appointment.start_time).getTime();\n    const appointmentEnd = new Date(appointment.end_time).getTime();\n    return nextStart < appointmentEnd && nextEnd > appointmentStart;\n  });\n}\n\nmodule.exports = {\n  toMinutes,\n  formatTime,\n  overlaps,\n  generateSlots,\n  getAvailableSlots,\n  isAppointmentConflict\n};\n"}},"id":"941b7b03-9eab-4b05-8526-4f106072dffa","timestamp":"2026-09-17T09:41:47.744Z","parentId":"81b8f8c7-3da0-4961-b64b-6e132f6ab920"}

{"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_JFTVgkwr09Whk8UIrgxq83Lf","toolName":"create_file","arguments":{"filePath":"/workspaces/medslot/server/src/controllers/clinicController.js","content":"const db = require('../database/db');\nconst { getAvailableSlots, isAppointmentConflict } = require('../services/schedulingEngine');\n\nfunction normalizeDoctorInput(payload) {\n  return {\n    name: String(payload.name || '').trim(),\n    specialization: String(payload.specialization || '').trim(),\n    start_time: payload.start_time || '09:00',\n    end_time: payload.end_time || '17:00',\n    break_start: payload.break_start || null,\n    break_end: payload.break_end || null,\n    appointment_duration: Number(payload.appointment_duration || 30),\n    buffer_minutes: Number(payload.buffer_minutes || 0)\n  };\n}\n\nfunction listDoctors(req, res) {\n  try {\n    const doctors = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM doctors ORDER BY name ASC').all();\n    res.json({ success: true, doctors });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('List doctors error:', error);\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to load doctors' });\n  }\n}\n\nfunction createDoctor(req, res) {\n  try {\n    const doctor = normalizeDoctorInput(req.body);\n\n    if (!doctor.name) {\n      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Doctor name is required' });\n    }\n\n    const result = db.prepare(`\n      INSERT INTO doctors (\n        name, specialization, start_time, end_time, break_start, break_end,\n        appointment_duration, buffer_minutes\n      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n    `).run(\n      doctor.name,\n      doctor.specialization || '',\n      doctor.start_time,\n      doctor.end_time,\n      doctor.break_start,\n      doctor.break_end,\n      doctor.appointment_duration,\n      doctor.buffer_minutes\n    );\n\n    const createdDoctor = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM doctors WHERE id = ?').get(result.lastInsertRowid);\n\n    res.status(201).json({ success: true, message: 'Doctor created', doctor: createdDoctor });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('Create doctor error:', error);\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to create doctor' });\n  }\n}\n\nfunction listPatients(req, res) {\n  try {\n    const patients = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM patients ORDER BY created_at DESC').all();\n    res.json({ success: true, patients });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('List patients error:', error);\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to load patients' });\n  }\n}\n\nfunction createPatient(req, res) {\n  try {\n    const { name, phone, email } = req.body;\n\n    if (!name || !phone) {\n      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Name and phone are required' });\n    }\n\n    const result = db.prepare(`\n      INSERT INTO patients (name, phone, email)\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n    `).run(name.trim(), phone.trim(), email ? email.trim() : null);\n\n    const patient = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM patients WHERE id = ?').get(result.lastInsertRowid);\n    res.status(201).json({ success: true, message: 'Patient created', patient });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('Create patient error:', error);\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to create patient' });\n  }\n}\n\nfunction listAppointments(req, res) {\n  try {\n    const { date } = req.query;\n    let query = `\n      SELECT a.*, d.name AS doctor_name, p.name AS patient_name\n      FROM appointments a\n      JOIN doctors d ON d.id = a.doctor_id\n      JOIN patients p ON p.id = a.patient_id\n    `;\n    const params = [];\n\n    if (date) {\n      query += ' WHERE date(a.start_time) = ?';\n      params.push(date);\n    }\n\n    query += ' ORDER BY a.start_time ASC';\n\n    const appointments = db.prepare(query).all(...params);\n    res.json({ success: true, appointments });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('List appointments error:', error);\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to load appointments' });\n  }\n}\n\nfunction getAvailability(req, res) {\n  try {\n    const { doctorId, date } = req.query;\n\n    if (!doctorId || !date) {\n      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'doctorId and date are required' });\n    }\n\n    const doctor = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM doctors WHERE id = ?').get(Number(doctorId));\n    if (!doctor) {\n      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Doctor not found' });\n    }\n\n    const appointments = db.prepare(`\n      SELECT * FROM appointments\n      WHERE doctor_id = ? AND date(start_time) = ? AND status != 'CANCELLED'\n      ORDER BY start_time ASC\n    `).all(Number(doctorId), date);\n\n    const slots = getAvailableSlots(doctor, appointments, date);\n    res.json({ success: true, doctor, date, slots });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('Get availability error:', error);\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to fetch availability' });\n  }\n}\n\nfunction createAppointment(req, res) {\n  try {\n    const { doctor_id, patient_id, date, time } = req.body;\n\n    if (!doctor_id || !patient_id || !date || !time) {\n      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Doctor, patient, date and time are required' });\n    }\n\n    const doctor = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM doctors WHERE id = ?').get(Number(doctor_id));\n    const patient = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM patients WHERE id = ?').get(Number(patient_id));\n\n    if (!doctor || !patient) {\n      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Doctor or patient not found' });\n    }\n\n    const startDateTime = new Date(`${date}T${time}:00`);\n    const durationMinutes = Number(doctor.appointment_duration || 30);\n    const endDateTime = new Date(startDateTime.getTime() + (durationMinutes * 60 * 1000));\n\n    const existingAppointments = db.prepare(`\n      SELECT * FROM appointments\n      WHERE doctor_id = ? AND status != 'CANCELLED' AND start_time < ? AND end_time > ?\n    `).all(Number(doctor_id), endDateTime.toISOString(), startDateTime.toISOString());\n\n    if (isAppointmentConflict(startDateTime.toISOString(), endDateTime.toISOString(), existingAppointments)) {\n      return res.status(409).json({ success: false, message: 'Selected time conflicts with an existing appointment' });\n    }\n\n    const result = db.prepare(`\n      INSERT INTO appointments (doctor_id, patient_id, start_time, end_time)\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\n    `).run(\n      Number(doctor_id),\n      Number(patient_id),\n      startDateTime.toISOString(),\n      endDateTime.toISOString()\n    );\n\n    const appointment = db.prepare(`\n      SELECT a.*, d.name AS doctor_name, p.name AS patient_name\n      FROM appointments a\n      JOIN doctors d ON d.id = a.doctor_id\n      JOIN patients p ON p.id = a.patient_id\n      WHERE a.id = ?\n    `).get(result.lastInsertRowid);\n\n    res.status(201).json({ success: true, message: 'Appointment booked', appointment });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('Create appointment error:', error);\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to book appointment' });\n  }\n}\n\nfunction cancelAppointment(req, res) {\n  try {\n    const { id } = req.params;\n    const { reason } = req.body;\n\n    const appointment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM appointments WHERE id = ?').get(Number(id));\n    if (!appointment) {\n      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'Appointment not found' });\n    }\n\n    const cancellationFee = 0;\n    const updated = db.prepare(`\n      UPDATE appointments\n      SET status = 'CANCELLED', cancellation_time = ?, cancellation_reason = ?, cancellation_fee = ?\n      WHERE id = ?\n    `).run(new Date().toISOString(), reason || 'Cancelled by staff', cancellationFee, Number(id));\n\n    if (updated.changes === 0) {\n      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Unable to cancel appointment' });\n    }\n\n    const cancelledAppointment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM appointments WHERE id = ?').get(Number(id));\n    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Appointment cancelled', appointment: cancelledAppointment });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('Cancel appointment error:', error);\n    res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to cancel appointment' });\n  }\n}\n\nmodule.exports = {\n  listDoctors,\n  createDoctor,\n  listPatients,\n  createPatient,\n  listAppointments,\n  getAvailability,\n  createAppointment,\n  cancelAppointment\n};\n"}},"id":"4c9d47ba-517b-476a-96a0-8973bc3cd300","timestamp":"2026-09-17T09:41:48.186Z","parentId":"50e13e27-91cf-45d3-b7be-7c607d1f396f"}

\"../public\")));\n\napp.get(\"/\", (req, res) => {\n  res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, \"../public/index.html\"));\n});\n\napp.get(\"/api/health\", (req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    success: true,\n    message: \"MediSlot API is running\",\n    timestamp: new Date().toISOString()\n  });\n});\n\napp.use(\"/api/auth\", authRoutes);\napp.use(\"/api\", clinicRoutes);\napp.use(\"/api/clinic\", clinicRoutes);\n\nconst PORT = process.env.PORT || 5000;\n\napp.listen(PORT, () => {\n  console.log(`🚀 MediSlot API running on port ${PORT}`);\n});"}]}},"id":"5e47c465-70ba-4c3f-9ec0-3d5c0b67448d","timestamp":"2026-09-17T09:43:03.437Z","parentId":"7899431c-9dcd-4943-b8c0-8d97361d3cc7"}

{"type":"tool.execution_complete","data":{"toolCallId":"call_vGEOGjpYnmPrg6AKh2JP3BA4","success":true},"id":"8b019f16-0270-499e-8e87-fc00f60ba7c5","timestamp":"2026-09-17T09:43:10.820Z","parentId":"4484d0e8-0e7e-4515-bda4-baf41f6fec42"}
{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"8d4523a8-1886-4081-a819-56f9b87be160","content":"","toolRequests":[{"toolCallId":"call_SGncKEnX7Peu0qRk4g6mmKlN","name":"create_file","arguments":"{\"filePath\":\"/workspaces/medslot/README.md\",\"content\":\"# MediSlot\\n\\nConflict-free clinic appointment scheduling system.\\n\\n## Overview\\n\\nMediSlot is a simple clinic scheduling application designed for front-desk staff. It supports doctor availability, patient tracking, appointment bookings, and cancellation workflows using a lightweight SQLite backend with an Express API.\\n\\n## Features\\n\\n- Staff authentication with JWT\\n- Doctor schedule configuration\\n- Patient record management\\n- Appointment creation with conflict detection\\n- Daily availability checks\\n- Cancellation tracking and policy support\\n- Built-in scheduling engine tests\\n\\n## Tech stack\\n\\n- Node.js + Express\\n- SQLite + better-sqlite3\\n- JWT authentication\\n- HTML/CSS static frontend\\n\\n## Project structure\\n\\n- `server/src/server.js` – application entry point\\n- `server/src/database/schema.js` – SQLite schema and seed data\\n- `server/src/services/schedulingEngine.js` – slot generation and conflict detection\\n- `server/src/controllers/` – API logic\\n- `server/src/routes/` – route definitions\\n- `server/public/index.html` – minimal browser UI\\n\\n## Setup\\n\\n1. Go to the server directory:\\n   `cd server`\\n2. Install dependencies:\\n   `npm install`\\n3. Start the API in development mode:\\n   `npm run dev`\\n4. Open the browser at:\\n   `http://localhost:5000`\\n\\n## Environment\\n\\nCreate a `.env` file in the `server` directory if you want to override defaults:\\n\\n```env\\nPORT=5000\\nJWT_SECRET=your-secret-key\\n```\\n\\n## API endpoints\\n\\n### Authentication\\n\\n- `POST /api/auth/register`\\n- `POST /api/auth/login`\\n\\n### Doctors\\n\\n- `GET /api/doctors`\\n- `POST /api/doctors`\\n\\n### Patients\\n\\n- `GET /api/patients`\\n- `POST /api/patients`\\n\\n### Appointments\\n\\n- `GET /api/appointments`\\n- `GET /api/appointments/availability?doctorId=1&date=2026-09-17`\\n- `POST /api/appointments`\\n- `PATCH /api/appointments/:id/cancel`\\n\\n## Reasoning\\n\\nThe appointment engine treats a slot as valid only when it fully fits within the working day and does not overlap a scheduled break or an existing appointment. This prevents double-booking while keeping schedule generation predictable for clinic staff.\\n\\n## Testing\\n\\nRun the regression tests with:\\n\\n```bash\\ncd server\\nnpm test\\n```\\n\"}","type":"function"}]},"id":"ab40f6cb-d631-43c4-bf55-70d579f34bef","timestamp":"2026-09-17T09:43:16.498Z","parentId":"8b019f16-0270-499e-8e87-fc00f60ba7c5"}

{"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_SGncKEnX7Peu0qRk4g6mmKlN","toolName":"create_file","arguments":{"filePath":"/workspaces/medslot/README.md","content":"# MediSlot\n\nConflict-free clinic appointment scheduling system.\n\n## Overview\n\nMediSlot is a simple clinic scheduling application designed for front-desk staff. It supports doctor availability, patient tracking, appointment bookings, and cancellation workflows using a lightweight SQLite backend with an Express API.\n\n## Features\n\n- Staff authentication with JWT\n- Doctor schedule configuration\n- Patient record management\n- Appointment creation with conflict detection\n- Daily availability checks\n- Cancellation tracking and policy support\n- Built-in scheduling engine tests\n\n## Tech stack\n\n- Node.js + Express\n- SQLite + better-sqlite3\n- JWT authentication\n- HTML/CSS static frontend\n\n## Project structure\n\n- `server/src/server.js` – application entry point\n- `server/src/database/schema.js` – SQLite schema and seed data\n- `server/src/services/schedulingEngine.js` – slot generation and conflict detection\n- `server/src/controllers/` – API logic\n- `server/src/routes/` – route definitions\n- `server/public/index.html` – minimal browser UI\n\n## Setup\n\n1. Go to the server directory:\n   `cd server`\n2. Install dependencies:\n   `npm install`\n3. Start the API in development mode:\n   `npm run dev`\n4. Open the browser at:\n   `http://localhost:5000`\n\n## Environment\n\nCreate a `.env` file in the `server` directory if you want to override defaults:\n\n```env\nPORT=5000\nJWT_SECRET=your-secret-key\n```\n\n## API endpoints\n\n### Authentication\n\n- `POST /api/auth/register`\n- `POST /api/auth/login`\n\n### Doctors\n\n- `GET /api/doctors`\n- `POST /api/doctors`\n\n### Patients\n\n- `GET /api/patients`\n- `POST /api/patients`\n\n### Appointments\n\n- `GET /api/appointments`\n- `GET /api/appointments/availability?doctorId=1&date=2026-09-17`\n- `POST /api/appointments`\n- `PATCH /api/appointments/:id/cancel`\n\n## Reasoning\n\nThe appointment engine treats a slot as valid only when it fully fits within the working day and does not overlap a scheduled break or an existing appointment. This prevents double-booking while keeping schedule generation predictable for clinic staff.\n\n## Testing\n\nRun the regression tests with:\n\n```bash\ncd server\nnpm test\n```\n"}},"id":"d6001a36-e7a8-41de-b598-950a568af2b6","timestamp":"2026-09-17T09:43:16.783Z","parentId":"91d6f834-1327-49f3-b148-7f28c8aa4f24"}

It supports doctor availability, patient tracking, appointment bookings, and cancellation workflows using a lightweight SQLite backend with an Express API.\n\n## Features\n\n- Staff authentication with JWT\n- Doctor schedule configuration\n- Patient record management\n- Appointment creation with conflict detection\n- Daily availability checks\n- Cancellation tracking and policy support\n- Built-in scheduling engine tests\n\n## Tech stack\n\n- Node.js + Express\n- SQLite + better-sqlite3\n- JWT authentication\n- HTML/CSS static frontend\n\n## Project structure\n\n- `server/src/server.js` – application entry point\n- `server/src/database/schema.js` – SQLite schema and seed data\n- `server/src/services/schedulingEngine.js` – slot generation and conflict detection\n- `server/src/controllers/` – API logic\n- `server/src/routes/` – route definitions\n- `server/public/index.html` – minimal browser UI\n\n## Setup\n\n1. Go to the server directory:\n   `cd server`\n2. Install dependencies:\n   `npm install`\n3. Start the API in development mode:\n   `npm run dev`\n4. Open the browser at:\n   `http://localhost:5000`\n\n## Environment\n\nCreate a `.env` file in the `server` directory if you want to override defaults:\n\n```env\nPORT=5000\nJWT_SECRET=your-secret-key\n```\n\n## API endpoints\n\n### Authentication\n\n- `POST /api/auth/register`\n- `POST /api/auth/login`\n\n### Doctors\n\n- `GET /api/doctors`\n- `POST /api/doctors`\n\n### Patients\n\n- `GET /api/patients`\n- `POST /api/patients`\n\n### Appointments\n\n- `GET /api/appointments`\n- `GET /api/appointments/availability?doctorId=1&date=2026-09-17`\n- `POST /api/appointments`\n- `PATCH /api/appointments/:id/cancel`\n\n## Reasoning\n\nThe appointment engine treats a slot as valid only when it fully fits within the working day and does not overlap a scheduled break or an existing appointment. This prevents double-booking while keeping schedule generation predictable for clinic staff.\n\n## Testing\n\nRun the regression tests with:\n\n```bash\ncd server\nnpm test\n```\n"}},"id":"25504b81-9d6d-4b85-8767-42548d40c379","timestamp":"2026-09-17T09:43:30.715Z","parentId":"8d67d0c7-4a83-4b65-8581-500377e2d859"}

{"type":"user.message","data":{"content":"Now proceed to PHASE 2: Database + Core REST APIs.\r\n\r\nThe scheduling engine has been verified with 5 passing tests. Keep the existing scheduling logic and do not regress it.\r\n\r\nBuild the backend completely before starting the frontend.\r\n\r\nGOALS:\r\n\r\n1. DATABASE\r\nUse the existing SQLite + better-sqlite3 setup.\r\n\r\nInspect the current schema first and improve it if necessary.\r\n\r\nRequired tables:\r\n- users\r\n- doctors\r\n- patients\r\n- appointments\r\n- cancellation_policies\r\n\r\nUse:\r\n- foreign keys\r\n- indexes for appointment lookup\r\n- sensible constraints\r\n- timestamps\r\n- appropriate status values\r\n\r\nDo not delete useful existing data or unnecessarily recreate the database.\r\n\r\n2. AUTHENTICATION\r\nImplement:\r\nPOST /api/auth/register\r\nPOST /api/auth/login\r\n\r\nUse bcryptjs for password hashing and JWT for authentication.\r\n\r\nCreate a reusable authentication middleware.\r\n\r\nProtected clinic-management endpoints should require a valid JWT.\r\n\r\nNever return password_hash in API responses.\r\n\r\n3. DOCTOR APIs\r\n\r\nImplement:\r\n\r\nGET /api/doctors\r\nPOST /api/doctors\r\nGET /api/doctors/:id\r\nPATCH /api/doctors/:id\r\n\r\nA doctor should support:\r\n- name\r\n- specialization\r\n- working start time\r\n- working end time\r\n- break start\r\n- break end\r\n- appointment duration\r\n- buffer minutes\r\n\r\nValidate that working hours and break times make sense.\r\n\r\n4. PATIENT APIs\r\n\r\nImplement:\r\n\r\nGET /api/patients\r\nPOST /api/patients\r\nGET /api/patients/:id\r\nGET /api/patients/search?q=\r\n\r\nSearch should support patient name and phone.\r\n\r\n5. APPOINTMENT APIs\r\n\r\nImplement:\r\n\r\nGET /api/appointments\r\nPOST /api/appointments\r\nGET /api/appointments/:id\r\nPATCH /api/appointments/:id\r\n\r\nSupport pagination and sorting.\r\n\r\nExample:\r\n\r\nGET /api/appointments?page=1&limit=10&sort=start_time&order=asc\r\n\r\nResponse should contain:\r\n\r\n{\r\n  data: [],\r\n  page: 1,\r\n  limit: 10,\r\n  total: 0,\r\n  totalPages: 0\r\n}\r\n\r\nOnly allow safe/whitelisted sort fields to prevent SQL injection.\r\n\r\n6. DOCTOR DAY VIEW API\r\n\r\nImplement:\r\n\r\nGET /api/doctors/:id/day?date=YYYY-MM-DD\r\n\r\nReturn:\r\n- doctor information\r\n- working hours\r\n- break\r\n- appointments for that day\r\n- appointment status\r\n- available slots\r\n- Slot Rescue opportunities if applicable\r\n\r\n7. AVAILABLE SLOTS API\r\n\r\nImplement:\r\n\r\nGET /api/doctors/:id/available-slots?date=YYYY-MM-DD\r\n\r\nUse the existing scheduling engine.\r\n\r\nSlots must be generated dynamically from:\r\n- working hours\r\n- break\r\n- appointment duration\r\n- buffer\r\n- active appointments\r\n\r\nDo not hard-code slots.\r\n\r\n8. APPOINTMENT BOOKING\r\n\r\nPOST /api/appointments must:\r\n\r\n- validate doctor\r\n- validate patient\r\n- validate start/end time\r\n- validate working hours\r\n- reject appointments during breaks\r\n- check existing ACTIVE appointments\r\n- use the overlap rule:\r\n\r\nnewStart < existingEnd && newEnd > existingStart\r\n\r\n- allow adjacent appointments\r\n- ignore CANCELLED appointments when checking conflicts\r\n- respect buffer time\r\n- return a useful conflict response\r\n\r\nFor a conflict response, include:\r\n- conflicting appointment\r\n- conflicting patient\r\n- existing time\r\n- requested time\r\n- suggested available slots\r\n\r\nExample:\r\n\r\n{\r\n  \"success\": false,\r\n  \"code\": \"SCHEDULE_CONFLICT\",\r\n  \"message\": \"Doctor is already booked during this time.\",\r\n  \"conflict\": {...},\r\n  \"suggestedSlots\": [...]\r\n}\r\n\r\n9. TRANSACTION SAFETY\r\n\r\nAppointment creation must be protected against race conditions as much as reasonably possible with SQLite.\r\n\r\nUse a database transaction around:\r\n- conflict check\r\n- appointment creation\r\n\r\nDo not rely only on frontend validation.\r\n\r\n10. CANCELLATION\r\n\r\nImplement:\r\n\r\nPOST /api/appointments/:id/cancel\r\n\r\nRules:\r\n- default free cancellation threshold = 4 hours\r\n- late cancellation fee = ₹100\r\n- these values must come from cancellation_policies\r\n- calculate based on appointment start time versus cancellation time\r\n- retain the appointment record\r\n- set status = CANCELLED\r\n- store cancellation_time\r\n- store cancellation_fee\r\n- store cancellation_reason\r\n\r\nReturn the calculated fee clearly.\r\n\r\n11. RESCHEDULING\r\n\r\nImplement:\r\n\r\nPOST /api/appointments/:id/reschedule\r\n\r\nThe endpoint must:\r\n- find available alternatives\r\n- prevent conflicts\r\n- respect working hours\r\n- respect breaks\r\n- respect buffer\r\n- never create overlapping active appointments\r\n- update the appointment safely\r\n\r\n12. SLOT RESCUE\r\n\r\nImplement a simple service that detects newly available slots caused by cancellations.\r\n\r\nDo not build an unnecessarily complicated AI system.\r\n\r\nReturn:\r\n- doctor\r\n- date\r\n- available slot\r\n- cancelled appointment that created the opportunity\r\n\r\nIf a waitlist structure does not yet exist, create a minimal waiting-list capability only if it can be done cleanly without delaying the core requirements.\r\n\r\n13. ERROR HANDLING\r\n\r\nCreate consistent API error responses.\r\n\r\nUse appropriate HTTP status codes:\r\n400 validation\r\n401 authentication\r\n403 authorization\r\n404 not found\r\n409 scheduling conflict\r\n500 unexpected error\r\n\r\nDo not expose internal stack traces to clients.\r\n\r\n14. TESTING\r\n\r\nExpand the backend test suite.\r\n\r\nTest at minimum:\r\n\r\n- registration\r\n- login\r\n- duplicate email\r\n- invalid login\r\n- doctor creation\r\n- patient creation\r\n- appointment creation\r\n- adjacent appointments\r\n- overlapping appointments\r\n- surrounding appointments\r\n- different doctors same time\r\n- cancelled appointment does not block slot\r\n- break restriction\r\n- working-hours restriction\r\n- buffer restriction\r\n- early cancellation = ₹0\r\n- late cancellation = ₹100\r\n- rescheduling conflict prevention\r\n- pagination\r\n- sorting\r\n- patient search\r\n\r\n15. CODE QUALITY\r\n\r\nFollow the existing architecture.\r\n\r\nKeep:\r\nroutes\r\ncontrollers\r\nservices\r\nmiddleware\r\ndatabase\r\nutils\r\n\r\nBusiness logic should not be placed directly inside route files.\r\n\r\nThe scheduling engine should remain reusable and testable independently from Express.\r\n\r\nIMPORTANT:\r\nDo not start building the React frontend yet.\r\n\r\nFirst:\r\n1. inspect current code\r\n2. implement Phase 2\r\n3. run the complete backend test suite\r\n4. fix every failure\r\n5. verify the server starts\r\n6. verify the health endpoint\r\n7. verify the core APIs\r\n8. report exactly what was implemented and tested\r\n\r\nDo not weaken tests or delete tests simply to make them pass.\r\n\r\nDo not stop at writing code. Actually run the tests.","attachments":[]},"id":"8a4f88e4-b1bd-472c-af07-b9a3b8360fc1","timestamp":"2026-09-17T09:45:26.955Z","parentId":"fb068073-0141-411d-9dc0-b08667036226"}

JSON.stringify(options.body) : undefined\\n  });\\n\\n  const text = await response.text();\\n  let data = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};\\n\\n  return { status: response.status, data };\\n}\\n\\ntest.before(async () => {\\n  server = startServer(0);\\n  await new Promise((resolve) => server.once('listening', resolve));\\n  baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;\\n});\\n\\ntest.after(async () => {\\n  if (server) await new Promise((resolve, reject) => server.close((error) => error ? reject(error) : resolve()));\\n});\\n\\ntest('registration works', async () => {\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/auth/register', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    body: { name: 'Clinic Staff', email: 'staff@example.com', password: 'secret123' }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 201);\\n  assert.equal(result.data.success, true);\\n  assert.ok(result.data.user?.id);\\n});\\n\\ntest('login works and returns JWT', async () => {\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/auth/login', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    body: { email: 'staff@example.com', password: 'secret123' }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 200);\\n  assert.equal(result.data.success, true);\\n  assert.ok(result.data.token);\\n  token = result.data.token;\\n});\\n\\ntest('duplicate email is rejected', async () => {\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/auth/register', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    body: { name: 'Clone User', email: 'staff@example.com', password: 'secret456' }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 409);\\n  assert.equal(result.data.success, false);\\n});\\n\\ntest('invalid login is rejected', async () => {\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/auth/login', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    body: { email: 'staff@example.com', password: 'wrongpass' }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 401);\\n  assert.equal(result.data.success, false);\\n});\\n\\ntest('doctor creation works', async () => {\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/doctors', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      name: 'Dr. Smith',\\n      specialization: 'Cardiology',\\n      start_time: '09:00',\\n      end_time: '17:00',\\n      break_start: '12:00',\\n      break_end: '12:30',\\n      appointment_duration: 30,\\n      buffer_minutes: 15\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 201);\\n  doctorId = result.data.doctor.id;\\n  assert.ok(doctorId);\\n});\\n\\ntest('patient creation works', async () => {\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/patients', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { name: 'John Doe', phone: '9876543210', email: 'john@example.com' }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 201);\\n  patientId = result.data.patient.id;\\n  assert.ok(patientId);\\n});\\n\\ntest('appointment creation works', async () => {\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/appointments', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      doctor_id: doctorId,\\n      patient_id: patientId,\\n      start_time: '2026-09-17T09:00:00',\\n      end_time: '2026-09-17T09:30:00'\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 201);\\n  appointmentId = result.data.appointment.id;\\n  assert.ok(appointmentId);\\n});\\n\\ntest('adjacent appointments are allowed when buffer permits', async () => {\\n  const patient2 = await apiFetch('/api/patients', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { name: 'Jane Doe', phone: '9876543211', email: 'jane@example.com' }\\n  });\\n\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/appointments', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      doctor_id: doctorId,\\n      patient_id: patient2.data.patient.id,\\n      start_time: '2026-09-17T09:45:00',\\n      end_time: '2026-09-17T10:15:00'\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 201);\\n});\\n\\ntest('overlapping appointments are rejected', async () => {\\n  const patient3 = await apiFetch('/api/patients', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { name: 'Sam Lee', phone: '9876543212', email: 'sam@example.com' }\\n  });\\n\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/appointments', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      doctor_id: doctorId,\\n      patient_id: patient3.data.patient.id,\\n      start_time: '2026-09-17T09:10:00',\\n      end_time: '2026-09-17T09:40:00'\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 409);\\n  assert.equal(result.data.code, 'SCHEDULE_CONFLICT');\\n});\\n\\ntest('surrounding appointments in different time windows are allowed', async () => {\\n  const patient4 = await apiFetch('/api/patients', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { name: 'Chris Ray', phone: '9876543213', email: 'chris@example.com' }\\n  });\\n\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/appointments', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      doctor_id: doctorId,\\n      patient_id: patient4.data.patient.id,\\n      start_time: '2026-09-17T10:30:00',\\n      end_time: '2026-09-17T11:00:00'\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 201);\\n});\\n\\ntest('different doctors can share the same time', async () => {\\n  const doctor2 = await apiFetch('/api/doctors', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { '13:00',\\n      break_end: '13:30',\\n      appointment_duration: 30,\\n      buffer_minutes: 0\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  const patient5 = await apiFetch('/api/patients', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { name: 'Noah Reed', phone: '9876543214', email: 'noah@example.com' }\\n  });\\n\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/appointments', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      doctor_id: doctor2.data.doctor.id,\\n      patient_id: patient5.data.patient.id,\\n      start_time: '2026-09-17T09:00:00',\\n      end_time: '2026-09-17T09:30:00'\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 201);\\n});\\n\\ntest('cancelled appointment does not block slot', async () => {\\n  const patient6 = await apiFetch('/api/patients', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { name: 'Ella West', phone: '9876543215', email: 'ella@example.com' }\\n  });\\n\\n  const create = await apiFetch('/api/appointments', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      doctor_id: doctorId,\\n      patient_id: patient6.data.patient.id,\\n      start_time: '2026-09-18T10:00:00',\\n      end_time: '2026-09-18T10:30:00'\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  const cancel = await apiFetch(`/api/appointments/${create.data.appointment.id}/cancel`, {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { reason: 'Patient rescheduled' }\\n  });\\n\\n  const slot = await apiFetch(`/api/doctors/${doctorId}/available-slots?date=2026-09-18`, {\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(cancel.status, 200);\\n  assert.equal(slot.status, 200);\\n  assert.ok(slot.data.slots.includes('10:00'));\\n});\\n\\ntest('break restriction is enforced', async () => {\\n  const patient7 = await apiFetch('/api/patients', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { name: 'Luke Hart', phone: '9876543216', email: 'luke@example.com' }\\n  });\\n\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/appointments', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      doctor_id: doctorId,\\n      patient_id: patient7.data.patient.id,\\n      start_time: '2026-09-18T12:15:00',\\n      end_time: '2026-09-18T12:45:00'\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 409);\\n  assert.equal(result.data.code, 'SCHEDULE_CONFLICT');\\n});\\n\\ntest('working-hours restriction is enforced', async () => {\\n  const patient8 = await apiFetch('/api/patients', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { name: 'Mia Lee', phone: '9876543217', email: 'mia@example.com' }\\n  });\\n\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/appointments', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      doctor_id: doctorId,\\n      patient_id: patient8.data.patient.id,\\n      start_time: '2026-09-18T08:00:00',\\n      end_time: '2026-09-18T08:30:00'\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 400);\\n  assert.equal(result.data.code, 'VALIDATION_ERROR');\\n});\\n\\ntest('buffer restriction is enforced', async 'noel@example.com' }\\n  });\\n\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/appointments', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      doctor_id: doctorId,\\n      patient_id: patient9.data.patient.id,\\n      start_time: '2026-09-17T09:40:00',\\n      end_time: '2026-09-17T10:10:00'\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 409);\\n});\\n\\ntest('early cancellation is free and late cancellation is ₹100', async () => {\\n  const patient10 = await apiFetch('/api/patients', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { name: 'Ruby Moss', phone: '9876543219', email: 'ruby@example.com' }\\n  });\\n\\n  const early = await apiFetch('/api/appointments', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      doctor_id: doctorId,\\n      patient_id: patient10.data.patient.id,\\n      start_time: '2026-09-19T15:00:00',\\n      end_time: '2026-09-19T15:30:00'\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  const earlyCancel = await apiFetch(`/api/appointments/${early.data.appointment.id}/cancel`, {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { reason: 'Patient request' }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(earlyCancel.status, 200);\\n  assert.equal(earlyCancel.data.fee, 0);\\n\\n  const late = await apiFetch('/api/appointments', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      doctor_id: doctorId,\\n      patient_id: patient10.data.patient.id,\\n      start_time: '2026-09-20T16:00:00',\\n      end_time: '2026-09-20T16:30:00'\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  const lateCancel = await apiFetch(`/api/appointments/${late.data.appointment.id}/cancel`, {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { reason: 'Late notice' }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(lateCancel.status, 200);\\n  assert.equal(lateCancel.data.fee, 100);\\n});\\n\\ntest('rescheduling prevents conflicts', async () => {\\n  const patient11 = await apiFetch('/api/patients', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: { name: 'Olivia Price', phone: '9876543220', email: 'olivia@example.com' }\\n  });\\n\\n  const appointment = await apiFetch('/api/appointments', {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },\\n    body: {\\n      doctor_id: doctorId,\\n      patient_id: patient11.data.patient.id,\\n      start_time: '2026-09-21T09:00:00',\\n      end_time: '2026-09-21T09:30:00'\\n    }\\n  });\\n\\n  const result = await apiFetch(`/api/appointments/${appointment.data.appointment.id}/reschedule`, {\\n    method: 'POST',\\n    headers: { Authorization: assert.ok(Array.isArray(result.data.data));\\n  assert.ok(result.data.total >= 1);\\n});\\n\\ntest('patient search works by name or phone', async () => {\\n  const result = await apiFetch('/api/patients/search?q=John', {\\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }\\n  });\\n\\n  assert.equal(result.status, 200);\\n return `${String(hours).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(minutes).padStart(2, '0')}`;\\n}\\n\\nfunction overlaps(startA, endA, startB, endB) {\\n  const firstStart = startA instanceof Date ? : new Date(startB).getTime();\\n  const secondEnd = endB instanceof Date ? endB.getTime() : new Date(endB).getTime();\\n\\n  if (Number.isNaN(firstStart) || Number.isNaN(firstEnd) || toMinutes(endB);\\n    return aStart < bEnd && aEnd > bStart;\\n  }\\n\\n  return firstStart < secondEnd && firstEnd > secondStart;\\n}\\n\\nfunction generateSlots(startTime, endTime, durationMinutes, const safeMinutes = ((totalMinutes % (24 * 60)) + (24 * 60)) % (24 * 60);\\n  const hours = Math.floor(safeMinutes / 60);\\n  const minutes = safeMinutes % 60;\\n  return `${String(hours).padStart(2, aStart = toMinutes(startA);\\n    const aEnd = toMinutes(endA);\\n    const bStart = toMinutes(startB);\\n    const bEnd = toMinutes(endB);\\n    return aStart < bEnd && aEnd > bStart;\\n  }\\n\\n  return firstStart < secondEnd && firstEnd > secondStart;\\n}\\n\\nfunction generateSlots(startTime, endTime, durationMinutes, breakStart, breakEnd) {\\n  const startMinutes = toMinutes(startTime);\\n
